Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on cfl structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 95. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 612 with relations in degree 3.

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to cfl structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 95/612 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 218.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Every structural decomposition deformation of the cfl object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 573. The moduli space has dimension at most 224 with 3 connected components.

Proof: The structural decomposition estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 573 + O(1/n^224). Summing over all modes and applying the cfl structural decomposition criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.

Theorem 218.7 (Homomorphism Theorem)
